# Research report — agentUsage

> **DO NOT MERGE.** Living research dump. Routines append here.

Project: terminal-first local dashboard for AI coding-agent / IDE / LLM API usage, spend, quotas, and rate limits. Auto-detects tools and keys. Public repo: https://github.com/nurulislamz/agentusage

## Competitors

### Seeded 2026-09-04 (v2)

**Primary peer risk:** [OpenUsage.sh](https://openusage.sh) markets nearly the same thesis (local terminal multi-tool quotas/spend/auto-detect). Differentiate on coverage, UX, or packaging — or collide.

| Product | Layer | What they do | Vs agentUsage |
|---|---|---|---|
| [OpenUsage.sh](https://openusage.sh) ([GitHub](https://github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage)) | Local multi-tool TUI | Auto-detect agents/keys, quotas, spend, rate limits, burn rate, statusline; 35+ tools. | **Nearest twin.** Treat as #1 peer. |
| [ccusage](https://github.com/ryoppippi/ccusage) | Local CLI reports | Daily/weekly/monthly/session/blocks from agent logs (Claude, Codex, OpenCode, Goose, Copilot CLI, Gemini, …). | Strong OSS peer on history/reports; thinner live quota dashboard. |
| ccusage UI dashboards / claude-monitor | Local UI/TUI | HTML or live burn-rate for Claude Code. | Claude-centric niche. |
| Cursor Spending tab + Admin API | Vendor | Plan pools, spend limits; Enterprise team spend APIs. | Cursor only. |
| [Cursor Spend Tracker](https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=helper2424.cursor-spend-tracker) | Extension | Status-bar spend via Admin API. | Cursor team only. |
| [cursor-usage-tracker](https://github.com/ofershap/cursor-usage-tracker) | Self-host team | Cursor Enterprise spend + anomaly Slack alerts. | FinOps for Cursor orgs, not local multi-provider autodetection. |
| Claude Code `/usage` `/cost`, claude.ai Usage | Vendor | Session/plan bars, 5h blocks. | Anthropic only. |
| OpenRouter Activity | Vendor / gateway | Credits and model cost analytics. | Hosted gateway billing. |
| [Helicone](https://www.helicone.ai), [Langfuse](https://langfuse.com) | Cloud/proxy observability | Traces, sessions, cost for instrumented apps. | Wrong layer for raw CLI agent logs; complementary if exporting OTel later. |
| [LiteLLM](https://docs.litellm.ai) proxy spend | Gateway | Virtual keys, budgets, admin UI. | Org gateway control, not personal agent detector. |
| Vantage Cursor reports / Lineman.io | FinOps / team SaaS | Cloud spend or Claude team visibility. | Enterprise; not terminal-first local. |

**Positioning**
- Narrative: **one pane for every agent on the machine**.
- Beat ccusage on live quotas/rate-limit probing + dashboard UX.
- Explicitly differentiate from OpenUsage.sh (providers, UX, install path) — same category.

Sources: openusage.sh; github.com/ryoppippi/ccusage; Cursor/Anthropic docs; Helicone/Langfuse/LiteLLM docs.

### Update 2026-09-04 (weekday scrape)

OpenUsage and ccusage both moved; twin-risk is sharper.

| Product | Delta | Vs agentUsage |
|---|---|---|
| [OpenUsage.sh](https://openusage.sh) | Now markets **34–36 providers**, local SQLite daemon, tmux status-bar + Claude Code statusline installers, headless daily/weekly/monthly/session/billing-block reports, Prometheus hub, explicit “vs Langfuse/Helicone” docs, brew/curl/go install. Coverage spans agents (Claude, Codex, Cursor, Copilot, Gemini, OpenCode, Ollama, Amp, Goose, Roo, Kilo, Kiro, Zed, …) + API platforms (OpenRouter, Groq, Mistral, DeepSeek, xAI, …). | Category definition is almost 1:1 with agentUsage. Must pick a wedge: deeper auto-detect reliability, better UX, niche providers, or packaging — not “we also do local quotas.” |
| [ccusage](https://github.com/ryoppippi/ccusage) ([ccusage org mirror](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage)) | Unified multi-source CLI; agents now include Amp, Droid, Codebuff, Hermes, pi, Goose, OpenClaw, Kilo, Kimi, Qwen, Copilot CLI, Gemini, plus newer **Antigravity / Grok Build / ZCode**. `blocks` for Claude 5h windows; LiteLLM-backed pricing; Nix sandbox pricing lock. | Remains the best **log-history / cost-report** OSS peer. Still thinner on live quota/rate-limit TUI + key autodetection. |
| OpenUsage docs vs observability | OpenUsage publishes a comparison framing Langfuse/Helicone as *hosted app observability*, itself as *local quota tracker*. | Keep Helicone/Langfuse/LiteLLM in “adjacent layer” — don’t pretend they’re desktop twins. |
| Vendor UIs | Cursor Spending / Claude `/usage`/`/cost` / OpenRouter Activity unchanged as single-vendor panes. | Still the fragmentation OpenUsage/agentUsage claim to fix. |

**Positioning refresh**
- Treat **OpenUsage.sh as the primary collision risk** (same install story, same auto-detect thesis).
- Beat **ccusage** on live quotas + multi-provider dashboard, not on historical report breadth (they’re expanding fast).
- Keep cloud observability (Helicone/Langfuse) and gateway spend (LiteLLM) as complementary, not competitors for the terminal autodetection use case.

Sources: [openusage.sh](https://openusage.sh/); [openusage.sh/llms.txt](https://openusage.sh/llms.txt); [github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage](https://github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage); [github.com/ryoppippi/ccusage](https://github.com/ryoppippi/ccusage); [github.com/ccusage/ccusage](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage); [openusage local-quota guide](https://openusage.sh/local-quota-tracker-for-claude-code-codex-cursor/).


### Update 2026-09-07

Fresh weekend scrape. Twin-risk with OpenUsage remains the headline; Splitrail is the clearest secondary local peer moving.

| Product | Delta | Vs agentUsage |
|---|---|---|
| [OpenUsage.sh](https://openusage.sh/) ([GitHub](https://github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage), ~187★) | Still markets **35 providers**, local SQLite daemon, tmux + Claude Code statusline, headless reports, Prometheus hub, auto-detect tools/keys. Newer docs emphasize **cost-attribution hooks** (`openusage integrations install` for Claude Code / Codex / OpenCode) to get per-turn/project rows beyond poll totals. Updated ~2026-09-05. | **Nearest twin** — category definition still ~1:1. Wedge must be reliability, UX, niche providers, or packaging — not “also local quotas.” |
| [ccusage](https://github.com/ryoppippi/ccusage) (~18.4k★) | Still the category CLI for historical daily/weekly/monthly/session/`blocks` reports across many agents (Claude, Codex, OpenCode, Amp, Droid, Hermes, pi, Goose, OpenClaw, Kilo, Kimi, Qwen, Copilot CLI, Gemini, …). Active today (updated 2026-09-07). | Best **log-history / cost-report** OSS peer; thinner live quota/rate-limit TUI + key autodetection. |
| [Splitrail](https://github.com/Piebald-AI/splitrail) (~219★) | Real-time cross-agent token/cost monitor; agent list now includes Antigravity, Zoo Code, Cline/Roo/Kilo, Copilot VS Code + CLI, OpenCode, Pi, Piebald. Optional **Splitrail Cloud** sync + **MCP server** (`get_daily_stats`, `get_cost_breakdown`, …). Updated ~2026-09-06. | Stronger secondary local peer than menu-bar wrappers; more “live monitor” than OpenUsage’s full quota dashboard thesis, but closing the gap. |
| Vendor UIs | Cursor Spending / Claude `/usage`/`/cost` / OpenRouter Activity unchanged as single-vendor panes. | Fragmentation OpenUsage/agentUsage claim to fix. |
| Helicone / Langfuse / LiteLLM | Still hosted observability / gateway spend layers. | Adjacent, not desktop twins — keep that framing. |

**Positioning refresh**
- **OpenUsage.sh** = primary collision (same install story + auto-detect + live quotas).
- **Splitrail** = rising real-time multi-agent monitor (+ MCP/cloud option) — watch as #2 local peer.
- **ccusage** = report/parser standard to stay compatible with; don’t try to out-breadth their historical CLI alone.
- Differentiate on live quota/rate-limit probing + auto-detect reliability + dashboard UX across *every* agent on the machine.

Sources: [openusage.sh](https://openusage.sh/); [openusage cost attribution](https://openusage.sh/docs/guides/cost-attribution/); [github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage](https://github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage); [github.com/ryoppippi/ccusage](https://github.com/ryoppippi/ccusage); [github.com/Piebald-AI/splitrail](https://github.com/Piebald-AI/splitrail); GitHub API star counts 2026-09-07.

### Update 2026-09-08

|---|---|---|
| [OpenUsage.sh](https://openusage.sh/) ([GitHub](https://github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage), ~187★) | Homepage still markets ~34–35 providers + local SQLite daemon + tmux/Claude statusline + auto-detect. Wedge must be reliability, UX, niche providers, or packaging. |
| [ccusage](https://github.com/ryoppippi/ccusage) / [ccusage.com](https://ccusage.com/) (~18.4k★) | Still the category CLI for historical daily/weekly/monthly/session/`blocks` across many agents. **Pushed today** (2026-09-08). | Best **log-history / cost-report** OSS peer; thinner live quota/rate-limit TUI + key autodetection. |

**Positioning refresh**

- **Splitrail** = rising real-time multi-agent monitor (+ MCP/cloud).

- Keep Helicone / Langfuse / LiteLLM / **AgentCost** as complementary observability/gateway spend — not desktop twins.

**Positioning refresh**
- OpenUsage.sh remains the primary collision but has now gone six days without product changes.
- The long tail of tiny quota CLIs (token-burn, aiquota, quota) all converge on the same three features: pace vs reset projection, JSON output, and a "low quota" alert/exit code. agentUsage should ship all three as table stakes, and consider OTel/Prometheus export like token-burn and OpenUsage.sh.
- Keep ccusage as the report/parser reference and tokscale as the client-coverage reference.

### Update 2026-10-06

Fresh Tuesday scrape (Europe/London / BST). **Biggest change today is a platform shift, not a competitor release:** from **6 Oct 2026**, new **Claude Cowork** tasks on Pro and Max run in **Anthropic's cloud**; the "Only on your computer" option is gone and scheduled tasks move to the cloud too ([Claude Help Center](https://support.claude.com/en/articles/15520349-use-claude-cowork-on-web-desktop-and-mobile)). Cowork still draws from the same five-hour session and weekly limits as Claude chat and Claude Code ([pricing FAQ](https://claude.com/pricing)). **codeburn** already documented the consequence (#1639, Mon ~16:23 BST): new Cowork tasks no longer write local session files, so log-parsing tools can't price them, while quota windows read from Anthropic's own usage figures still include them. Any tracker that only parses local logs will now under-count Claude usage for Cowork users.

Competitor releases since Mon:
- **[openusage.ai](https://github.com/robinebers/openusage)** promoted **[v0.7.14](https://github.com/robinebers/openusage/releases/tag/v0.7.14)** to stable (~05:20 BST Tue): independent Codex homes with token refresh, restored multi-account Codex local spend, **structured Cursor team usage pools**, slow Codex history scans no longer block live quota, OpenCode reset countdowns below 1%, "Report an Issue" menu. **4,322★** (+9).
- **[codeburn](https://github.com/getagentseal/codeburn)** main (Mon): **syncs Cursor usage from cursor.com automatically** (#1637) with a sync-status / on-off switch in the apps (#1638); local Cursor dollars and Agent-transcript costs now labelled **estimates, not the bill** (#1615, #1634); Codex Fast / Flex / Auto Review pricing (#1628, #1641, #1642); `compare` skips a provider whose discovery fails instead of exiting (#1643). Tag still **0.9.25**; **11,345★** (+6).
- **[CodeZeno Monitor](https://github.com/CodeZeno/Claude-Code-Usage-Monitor)** **[v2.18.1](https://github.com/CodeZeno/Claude-Code-Usage-Monitor/releases/tag/v2.18.1)** (~10:09 BST Mon): Codex individual limits in the poller. **572★** (+6).
- **[pane](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane)** **[v0.4.57](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane/releases/tag/v0.4.57)** (~21:11 BST Mon): Codex spend from Orca-managed `CODEX_HOME`s and **extra synced session folders** so usage from other machines counts. **68★**.
- **[QuotaBar](https://github.com/majiayu000/quotabar)** main (Mon, untagged after **v0.5.9**): **warns when a window will run out before its reset**, points you to another account when the headline runs low, shows each plan's **API-equivalent value** and today's API-equivalent cost; hides Antigravity until its quota can be tracked. **53★**.
- **Langfuse** **[v4.51.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.51.0)** (Mon) then **[v4.52.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.52.0)** (~09:07 BST Tue): AI-gateway relay of OpenAI Chat Completions **with usage telemetry**, gateway request IDs, org usage breakdown on every deployment. **35,423★**.
- Holding: **OpenUsage.sh** still no product commits (Mon = Dependabot docs/website bumps only), **v0.25.0**, **217★** (+1); **ccusage** **v20.0.26**, pricing-snapshot churn, **18,890★** (+15); **tokscale** **v4.18.0**, **5,631★** (+6); **Splitrail** **v3.10.3**; **Syrtis** **v2.4.0**, **405★** (+3); **LiteLLM** Latest **v1.104.0** / **v1.105.0-rc.1**, **60,203★**; **Helicone** quiet day **20**; aiusage **v1.5.19**; token-burn / aiquota / quota idle.

**Positioning refresh**
- The Cowork change is an opening: agentUsage can be the tool that tells users *"your local logs show X, but Anthropic says you've used Y of your window — the difference is cloud work"* instead of silently under-reporting.
- codeburn's cursor.com sync and "estimate, not the bill" labelling, openusage.ai's Cursor team pools, and QuotaBar's run-out-before-reset warning are the three features to match next.
- pane's "extra session folders from other machines" points at multi-machine aggregation as a differentiator for a local-first tool.

Sources: [support.claude.com Cowork on web/desktop/mobile](https://support.claude.com/en/articles/15520349-use-claude-cowork-on-web-desktop-and-mobile); [claude.com/pricing](https://claude.com/pricing); [support.claude.com usage limit best practices](https://support.claude.com/en/articles/9797557-usage-limit-best-practices); [getagentseal/codeburn commits](https://github.com/getagentseal/codeburn/commits) (#1637, #1638, #1639, #1615, #1634, #1643); [robinebers/openusage v0.7.14](https://github.com/robinebers/openusage/releases/tag/v0.7.14); [CodeZeno v2.18.1](https://github.com/CodeZeno/Claude-Code-Usage-Monitor/releases/tag/v2.18.1); [ItsJazii/pane v0.4.57](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane/releases/tag/v0.4.57); [majiayu000/quotabar commits](https://github.com/majiayu000/quotabar/commits); [langfuse v4.51.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.51.0) / [v4.52.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.52.0); [janekbaraniewski/openusage](https://github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage); [ccusage/ccusage](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage); [junhoyeo/tokscale](https://github.com/junhoyeo/tokscale); [Piebald-AI/splitrail](https://github.com/Piebald-AI/splitrail); [Nanako0129/syrtis](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis); [BerriAI/litellm](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm); [Helicone/helicone](https://github.com/Helicone/helicone); web search + GitHub API 2026-10-06 (Europe/London).

### Update 2026-10-08

Fresh Thursday scrape (Europe/London / BST), compared with Tue 6 Oct (no Wed entry was committed). **Biggest finding is a gap in this dump, not a new launch:** **[CodexBar](https://github.com/steipete/CodexBar)** by Peter Steinberger (MIT, macOS menu bar, created Nov 2025) has **22,304★** — more than ccusage — and had never been catalogued here. It shipped **[v0.73.0](https://github.com/steipete/CodexBar/releases/tag/v0.73.0)** on Wed (~13:26 BST): Langdock as **provider #91**, a Usage & Spend view that rolls long ranges up by week or month with drill-down to days and an hourly single-day view, a selectable statistics time zone, Codex long-context and Priority pricing fixes, managed Codex accounts from the CLI, and **Linux snapshots** exposing plan, balances, reset credits and spend. It also has a Rust CLI port, **[caut](https://github.com/Dicklesworthstone/coding_agent_usage_tracker)** (**89★**), that prints 16+ providers as tables or JSON/Markdown for agents to read. Between them they cover most of agentUsage's pitch except a terminal dashboard on Linux/Windows that also covers API keys.

Other movement since Tue:
- **[Syrtis](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis)** **v2.4.1** then **[v2.5.0](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis/releases/tag/v2.5.0)** (Tue ~19:53 / ~21:30 BST): downloads **Cursor usage itself** using the Cursor app's login (opt-in card, syncs at launch and every 30 min, switch-off deletes the data), no longer needing the tokscale CLI. **408★** (+3). Third tool this week to pull Cursor numbers from cursor.com (after codeburn and openusage.ai).
- **[ClaudeBar](https://github.com/tddworks/ClaudeBar)** **[v0.5.10](https://github.com/tddworks/ClaudeBar/releases/tag/v0.5.10)** (Thu ~07:04 BST): Codex no longer sticks on "Syncing…" (15 s timeout then terminal fallback), leaderboard upload retries. **1,531★**. README now lists 20 providers, notch / Touch Bar / iPhone widgets and script-based extensions.
- **[codeburn](https://github.com/getagentseal/codeburn)** main (Wed night–Thu ~05:54 BST): group projects by git repository (#1691, #1692), fold subagent sessions into their parent (#1690), attribute Claude calls to their own cwd (#1689), Grok per-request usage from `logs/unified.jsonl` (#1677), stop double-billing Cline cache tokens (#1675), Google AI plan presets (#1683), signed/notarised menu bar with one-click updates (#1684, #1685), and a doc on Claude Code usage it can't see in transcripts (#1693). Tag still **0.9.25**; **11,353★**.
- **[Splitrail](https://github.com/Piebald-AI/splitrail)** **[v3.11.0](https://github.com/Piebald-AI/splitrail/releases/tag/v3.11.0)** (Wed ~19:16 BST): Gemini 4 Argon and Claude Haiku 5.5 pricing, TUI per-period token totals. **222★**.
- **[pane](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane)** **[v0.4.58](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane/releases/tag/v0.4.58)** (Wed ~15:29 BST): Codex Auto-review counted at **$0** for ChatGPT sign-ins from 6 Oct. **71★** (+3).
- **[QuotaBar](https://github.com/majiayu000/quotabar)** main (Wed): exposes **incomplete usage and session-source evidence** (#211). **53★**.
- **[MeterBar](https://github.com/VincentShipsIt/meterbar.dev)** (**new**, 8★, v1.9.0 3 Oct): macOS menu bar for Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, OpenRouter and Grok; provider status checks so you can tell an outage from an exhausted quota; can **resume blocked sessions once a limit resets**; `meterbar usage --json`.
- **[ahsanhabibakik/aiusage](https://github.com/ahsanhabibakik/aiusage)** (**new**, 8★, v0.4.0 Jul): cross-platform terminal + tray tracker for Claude Code and Codex with a loopback JSON API (`127.0.0.1:8737/v1/usage`) and a Claude Code statusLine installer. Second "aiusage" naming collision after juliantanx/aiusage.
- **Langfuse** **v4.53.0** (Tue) and **[v4.54.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.54.0)** (Wed ~09:55 BST), Claude Haiku 5.5 pricing added; **35,514★**. **LiteLLM** Latest now **v1.104.2** (Thu ~09:39 BST) plus **v1.106.0-dev.2** prerelease; Lens gateway-cost refresh fix (#45105); **60,342★**.
- Holding: **OpenUsage.sh** no pushes since Mon, **v0.25.0**, **217★**; **ccusage** **v20.0.26**, hourly pricing snapshots, **18,915★**; **tokscale** **v4.18.0**, **5,639★**; **openusage.ai** **v0.7.14**, **4,331★**; **CodeZeno** **v2.18.1**, **575★**; **Helicone** quiet day 22 (last push 16 Sep), **6,207★**; token-burn / aiquota / quota idle.

**Positioning refresh**
- CodexBar's 22k stars mean the "one place for every coding-agent quota" idea is proven and well served on macOS. agentUsage's clearest lane is the terminal on any OS, plus LLM API keys and gateways (OpenRouter, LiteLLM) alongside the subscription quotas, which the menu-bar apps mostly skip.
- Features converging across peers this week: pulling Cursor usage from cursor.com (Syrtis, codeburn, openusage.ai), flagging when data is incomplete (QuotaBar, codeburn #1693), and separating a provider outage from your own exhausted quota (MeterBar).

| [Langfuse](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse) (**35,742★**) / [Helicone](https://github.com/Helicone/helicone) (**6,215★**) / [LiteLLM](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm) (**61,122★**) | Langfuse **v4.57/4.58**; LiteLLM FOCUS export + cache billing fixes. | Cloud observability / gateway layer: complementary. |

**Takeaway:** The terminal lane added three more entrants in a day. **quota-axi** is the notable one: it treats the *agent* as the reader (compact output, ~19 providers, no routing), which suggests agentUsage should ship a stable `--json` feed that agents and statuslines can consume, not just a human TUI. usagenometer already pairs quotas with a token ledger and run-out alerts, so agentUsage's edge has to stay **breadth of autodetection (IDEs + CLIs + raw API keys) plus real spend**, with forecasts treated as baseline.

Sources: [ClaudeBar v0.5.15](https://github.com/tddworks/ClaudeBar/releases/tag/v0.5.15); [Langfuse v4.57.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.57.0) / [v4.58.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.58.0); [CodexBar commits](https://github.com/steipete/CodexBar/commits/main); [codeburn commits](https://github.com/getagentseal/codeburn/commits/main); [LiteLLM commits](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/commits/main); [quota-axi README](https://github.com/kunchenguid/quota-axi/blob/main/README.md); [usagenometer on docs.rs](https://docs.rs/crate/usagenometer/latest) + crates.io API; [tokenusage](https://github.com/hanbu97/tokenusage); GitHub API stars/releases/commits 2026-10-13 (Europe/London).

**Takeaway:** CodexBar's v0.75.0 raises the bar on *how* credentials are found (every browser, minimal Keychain prompts) and adds real-time API throughput from OpenAI's Admin Usage API, so "auto-detect tools and keys" now has to include browser sessions and admin-key usage feeds, not just CLI auth files. The new small monitors (cctop, Quota Watch) both ship `--json`, reinforcing yesterday's point that a stable machine-readable feed is table stakes. agentUsage's edge remains cross-platform terminal-first breadth (IDEs + CLIs + raw API keys) with spend and quotas in one view.

**Takeaway:** Today's releases are mostly upkeep, and that is the signal: ccusage had to add zstd decoding because Codex started compressing its rollout logs, tokscale had to prefer Keychain over a stale credentials file, and Langfuse had to fix a Claude cache-read price. Any tool that parses local logs and prices tokens carries this treadmill. agentUsage should lean on shared pricing data and vendor usage endpoints where they exist, and keep its log parsers small and well-tested. CodexBar adding Anthropic API credit balances also narrows the "raw API keys" gap, so breadth across IDEs, CLIs and API keys on Linux/Windows as well as macOS stays agentUsage's clearest edge.

Sources: [CodexBar commits](https://github.com/steipete/CodexBar/commits/main); [ccusage v20.0.32](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage/releases/tag/v20.0.32) / [v20.0.33](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage/releases/tag/v20.0.33); [tokscale v4.19.0](https://github.com/junhoyeo/tokscale/releases/tag/v4.19.0) / [v4.19.1](https://github.com/junhoyeo/tokscale/releases/tag/v4.19.1); [quota-axi v0.1.62](https://github.com/kunchenguid/quota-axi/releases/tag/quota-axi-v0.1.62); [Langfuse v4.59.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.59.0); [LiteLLM releases](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/releases); [cctop-tui on PyPI](https://pypi.org/project/cctop-tui/); [token-burn](https://github.com/durandom/token-burn); [aiusage](https://github.com/ahsanhabibakik/aiusage); [ai-usage-tui](https://github.com/SophanaSok/ai-usage-tui); GitHub API stars/releases/commits 2026-10-15 (Europe/London); web search 2026-10-15.

| [ClaudeBar](https://github.com/tddworks/ClaudeBar) (**1,542★**) / [pane](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane) (**76★**) | ClaudeBar pause + OpenCode leaderboard; pane Gemini 4 Argon + spend UI. | Menu-bar / tray peers; multi-account UX bar rising. |
| [ccusage](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage) (**19,024★**) / [tokscale](https://github.com/junhoyeo/tokscale) (**5,694★**) | ccusage Antigravity→Argon map; tokscale Windows/Copilot fixes on main. | Log-parser references; model-ID churn continues. |
| [quota-axi](https://github.com/kunchenguid/quota-axi) (**154★**) | **v0.1.63** claude-swap accounts. | Agent-facing quota feed; multi-account Claude via swap tools. |
| [Token Use](https://github.com/russmckendrick/tokenuse) (**37★**, new) | Local TUI/desktop session archive + opt-in web quota sync. | Another spend-by-project TUI; thinner live multi-provider quotas than CodexBar/OpenUsage. |
| [cctop](https://github.com/SauersML/cctop) / [Quota Watch](https://github.com/lazydao/quota-watch) / [token-burn](https://github.com/durandom/token-burn) / [aiusage](https://github.com/ahsanhabibakik/aiusage) / [ai-usage-tui](https://github.com/SophanaSok/ai-usage-tui) / [llmquota](https://github.com/0xNyk/llmquota) / [usagenometer](https://docs.rs/crate/usagenometer/latest) / [quotas](https://github.com/clankercode/quotas) / [agent-quota](https://github.com/Halven08/agent-quota) / [caut](https://github.com/Dicklesworthstone/coding_agent_usage_tracker) | Quiet. | **Direct terminal twins**. |
| [OpenUsage.sh](https://openusage.sh/) ([GitHub](https://github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage), **226★**) | Quiet. | Nearest twin by design. |
| [codeburn](https://github.com/getagentseal/codeburn) (**11,468★**) / [openusage.ai](https://github.com/robinebers/openusage) / [CodeZeno](https://github.com/CodeZeno/Claude-Code-Usage-Monitor) (**616★**) / [QuotaBar](https://github.com/majiayu000/quotabar) / [Syrtis](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis) / [MeterBar](https://github.com/VincentShipsIt/meterbar.dev) | Stars only / nightly republish. | Menu-bar / Claude monitors. |
| [Langfuse](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse) (**35,899★**) / [Helicone](https://github.com/Helicone/helicone) / [LiteLLM](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm) (**61,368★**) | Langfuse **v4.60–4.61** gateway + skills; LiteLLM still **v1.105.1** stable. | Cloud observability / gateway: complementary. |

**Takeaway:** Splitrail adding a localhost web dashboard and CodexBar tagging Claude Platform API credits both pull peers toward "full local dashboard + API spend," which is agentUsage's stated lane. Multi-account UX (ClaudeBar Pause, quota-axi claude-swap) is the other live theme. agentUsage should keep breadth across IDEs/CLIs/API keys on Linux/Windows as well as macOS, ship a stable machine-readable quota feed, and treat model-ID / pricing alias churn (ccusage Argon map, pane rate card) as ongoing maintenance rather than one-off fixes. Token Use is another local spend TUI to watch, but it is archive-first rather than live multi-vendor quotas.

Sources: [CodexBar v0.76.0](https://github.com/steipete/CodexBar/releases/tag/v0.76.0); [Splitrail v3.12.0](https://github.com/Piebald-AI/splitrail/releases/tag/v3.12.0); [ClaudeBar v0.5.16](https://github.com/tddworks/ClaudeBar/releases/tag/v0.5.16); [ccusage v20.0.34](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage/releases/tag/v20.0.34); [quota-axi v0.1.63](https://github.com/kunchenguid/quota-axi/releases/tag/quota-axi-v0.1.63); [pane v0.4.60](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane/releases/tag/v0.4.60); [Langfuse v4.60.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.60.0) / [v4.61.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.61.0); [LiteLLM releases](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/releases); [Token Use](https://github.com/russmckendrick/tokenuse); [tokscale commits](https://github.com/junhoyeo/tokscale/commits/main); GitHub API stars/releases/commits 2026-10-16 (Europe/London); web / DDG search 2026-10-16.

### Update 2026-10-17

Fresh Saturday scrape (Europe/London / BST), compared with Fri 16 Oct. Biggest move: **CodexBar [v0.77.0](https://github.com/steipete/CodexBar/releases/tag/v0.77.0)**, one day after v0.76.0, adds an **HTTP usage server** in Settings and an opt-in cache so `codexbar usage` works **over SSH with a locked keychain** — both edge toward agentUsage's terminal/local-API lane. Also landed: **Syrtis [v2.8.0](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis/releases/tag/v2.8.0)** (DeepSeek Harness logs), **quota-axi [v0.1.64](https://github.com/kunchenguid/quota-axi/releases/tag/quota-axi-v0.1.64)**, **Langfuse [v4.62.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.62.0)** / **[v4.63.0](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse/releases/tag/v4.63.0)**. Two new terminal twins found: **[tokensleft](https://github.com/tokensleft/tokensleft)** and **[costsee](https://github.com/lef237/costsee)**.

Movement since Fri:
- **[CodexBar](https://github.com/steipete/CodexBar)** **[v0.77.0](https://github.com/steipete/CodexBar/releases/tag/v0.77.0)** (Fri ~18:36 BST): power/memory (Macs idle-sleep again while the Claude CLI source is active; up to 40% idle CPU cut; lighter Codex/Claude history scans); Claude usage from the **Claude desktop app's signed-in session**; per-token-account Claude config dirs for local cost; claude-swap session forecasts and **tcr** account pools; explicit **Gemini app** source for 5-hour/weekly limits; **Munito** and **Future AGI** spend providers; **Auto** ranks quotas by time-to-exhaustion; Spend layout items; six-day work week; **HTTP usage server**; opt-in app cache for SSH. **22,563→22,582★**.
- **[Syrtis](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis)** **[v2.8.0](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis/releases/tag/v2.8.0)** (Fri ~17:58 BST): reads **DeepSeek Harness** sessions under `~/.dsh/sessions` on its own tab (no limits card); refresh indicator waits for the local-usage read and shows "Reading local usage…" instead of false zeros. **414★**.
- **[quota-axi](https://github.com/kunchenguid/quota-axi)** **[v0.1.64](https://github.com/kunchenguid/quota-axi/releases/tag/quota-axi-v0.1.64)** (Fri ~15:13 BST): TUI names account lanes via `tui.accountLabels`. **154→155★**.

- **New — [tokensleft](https://github.com/tokensleft/tokensleft)** ([npm](https://www.npmjs.com/package/tokensleft), **2★**, v0.9.0, last push 14 Oct): Node TUI that **auto-detects** logged-in Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Kimi Code, Copilot, Grok, Antigravity, OpenCode and z.ai; reset times, **burn-rate forecasts**, threshold alerts; local per-CLI token + estimated API cost from logs with a LiteLLM/models.dev price snapshot. Closest design match to agentUsage's auto-detect pitch so far, tiny audience.
- **New — [costsee](https://github.com/lef237/costsee)** (**0★**, v0.1.5, Aug): Rust TUI for Codex (App Server windows), Claude Code (official status-line feed it installs) and OpenCode (local cost vs configured limits); non-interactive table report. Quota-only, three tools.

**Takeaway:** CodexBar shipping a local HTTP usage server and SSH-capable CLI in back-to-back releases means the biggest player now covers headless and scripted use, not just the macOS menu bar. agentUsage's remaining edges are Linux/Windows-first terminal UX, API-key spend alongside subscription quotas in one view, and a stable documented local feed. New twins tokensleft and costsee show the auto-detect + burn-rate TUI idea is easy to copy, so breadth of detected tools and correctness of pricing data matter more than the TUI itself.

### Update 2026-10-18

Fresh Sunday scrape (Europe/London / BST), compared with Sat 17 Oct. A busy Saturday evening of point releases: **tokscale [v4.20.0](https://github.com/junhoyeo/tokscale/releases/tag/v4.20.0)** (calendar-period analytics, cost provenance in JSON, Codex compaction usage), **ccusage [v20.0.35](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage/releases/tag/v20.0.35)** (stored cost in JSON model breakdowns), **pane [v0.4.61](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane/releases/tag/v0.4.61)** (OpenCode 2 support), **Syrtis [v2.9.0](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis/releases/tag/v2.9.0)**, and **LiteLLM [v1.106.0](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/releases/tag/v1.106.0)** stable on Sunday morning. **ClaudeBar** main rewired data sources overnight (vendored SQLite + QuickJS on every platform, Windows process port). New finds: **[VibeCodingTracker](https://github.com/Mai0313/VibeCodingTracker)** (Rust CLI/TUI) and two VS Code extensions, **Agent Stats** and **Universal Agent Quota Tracker**.

Movement since Sat:
- **[tokscale](https://github.com/junhoyeo/tokscale)** **[v4.20.0](https://github.com/junhoyeo/tokscale/releases/tag/v4.20.0)** (Sat ~22:12 BST): calendar periods + group reporting in analytics; **cost provenance exposed in JSON usage aggregates**; Codex usage counted from compaction turns and large compressed rollouts; Cursor incremental sync with isolated per-account caches; Copilot gh-host credential resolution; Amp server-side thread sync; native **Aside** browser usage; sub-cent cost/M shown as `<$0.01`; Goose Windows DB discovery; ambiguous-width terminal detection. **5,698→5,707★**.
- **[ccusage](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage)** **[v20.0.35](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage/releases/tag/v20.0.35)** (Sat ~18:23 BST): adapters report **stored cost** in JSON model breakdowns. **19,039→19,049★**.
- **[pane](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane)** **[v0.4.61](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane/releases/tag/v0.4.61)** (Sat ~21:16 BST): **OpenCode 2** (session_message usage, credential-table logins, incremental spend scans); Antigravity prefers prod Cloud Code, merges quota pools across hosts, shows Gemini 4 Argon weekly quota; Claude 429 backoff treats `Retry-After: 0` as absent; Droid keeps last good numbers when login expires; AIHubMix and DeepSeek Harness spend routed to their cards. **77★**.
- **[Syrtis](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis)** **[v2.9.0](https://github.com/Nanako0129/syrtis/releases/tag/v2.9.0)** (Sat ~18:39 BST): menu-bar gauge leads with a letter for the tracked window length (H ≤6h, D ~day, W ~week, M ~month), following Auto's least-left window. **414→415★**.
- **[ClaudeBar](https://github.com/tddworks/ClaudeBar)** main (Sun ~04:44–09:13 BST, untagged; still **v0.5.16**): one vendored **QuickJS + SQLite** on every platform; reads **OpenCode's database directly** so v1 and v2 both work; Processes port runs commands/scripts/JSON-RPC on **Windows**; accounts show only what their own sign-in reaches; docs regrouped by bounded context. **1,546★**.
- **[LiteLLM](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm)** **[v1.106.0](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/releases/tag/v1.106.0)** stable (Sun ~06:53 BST; **v1.107.0-rc.1** at ~09:00 BST): one-command Docker quickstart (gateway + Postgres + admin UI); pass-through spend logs carry key / team / project tags; daily model-usage writes batched; readable model leaderboard chart; stdio MCP servers disabled by default. **61,415→61,464★**.
- **New — [VibeCodingTracker](https://github.com/Mai0313/VibeCodingTracker)** (**14★**, v2.7.1 Sep, last push 15 Oct): lightweight Rust CLI/TUI for real-time cost + token analytics across Claude Code, Codex, Copilot, Gemini and (per its README) OpenCode, Cursor, Hermes, Grok and DeepSeek Harness. Another log-parser twin.
- **New — [Agent Stats](https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=agentstats.agentstats)** (VS Code, **301** installs, v0.3.4 Jan 2026): analytics dashboard for Claude Code + Codex CLI usage/tokens/cost inside the editor. **New — [Universal Agent Quota Tracker](https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=TCTinh.universal-agent-quota-tracker)** (VS Code, **875** installs, v0.1.6 Jan 2026): quota monitor for OpenCode Antigravity Auth, Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI and Z.AI. Both are editor-embedded and dormant since January; useful as the "IDE status bar" alternative to a terminal dashboard.
- Also seen: [Ian Paterson's bash script](https://ianlpaterson.com/blog/tracking-claude-codex-gemini-quotas-from-one-script/) normalising Claude OAuth / Codex JSON-RPC / Gemini session quotas into one JSON on an hourly cron — evidence that power users want a single machine-readable quota file, which agentUsage can ship natively.

**Takeaway:** The week's theme continues — peers converge on the same breadth (OpenCode 2, Antigravity/Argon, Windows) and are now adding *cost provenance* (tokscale, ccusage) so users know whether a number is reported or estimated. agentUsage should make provenance and quota-window labels first-class in its terminal view and JSON feed, and keep API-key spend plus subscription quotas in one place, since that combination is still rarer than any single feature.

## Open source

### Seeded 2026-09-04

Peers and libraries for a local multi-agent usage/spend/quota dashboard: **log parsers**, **live TUIs**, **gateway cost**, **OTel observability**, **Cursor/Claude/Codex parsers**.

| Repo | Stars | License | Why it matters |
|---|---:|---|---|
| [ccusage/ccusage](https://github.com/ccusage/ccusage) | ~18k | (check / npm) | Category-defining CLI: daily/session/blocks cost from local agent JSONL (Claude, Codex, Copilot CLI, Gemini, …). Primary report-format peer. |
| [janekbaraniewski/openusage](https://github.com/janekbaraniewski/openusage) | ~186 | MIT | Local multi-tool quota/spend TUI + SQLite — **nearest product twin** (same thesis as agentUsage). |
| [Piebald-AI/splitrail](https://github.com/Piebald-AI/splitrail) | ~217 | MIT | Cross-platform real-time token/cost monitor across many coding agents. |
| [cobra91/better-ccusage](https://github.com/cobra91/better-ccusage) | ~81 | MIT | Faster multi-provider ccusage-style analyzer — watch for format forks. |
| [ofershap/cursor-usage-tracker](https://github.com/ofershap/cursor-usage-tracker) | ~33 | MIT | Self-hosted Cursor Enterprise spend + anomaly alerts — team FinOps, not local autodetection. |
| [BerriAI/litellm](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm) | ~58k | (check) | AI gateway with spend tracking / virtual keys — pricing tables useful; wrong layer for personal agent logs. |
| [langfuse/langfuse](https://github.com/langfuse/langfuse) | ~34k | (check) | OSS LLM observability + OTel — complementary if exporting traces later. |
| [Helicone/helicone](https://github.com/Helicone/helicone) | ~6.1k | Apache-2.0 | OSS LLM observability proxy — same adjacent layer as Langfuse. |

**Also watch (ccusage ecosystem)**
- [sculptdotfun/viberank](https://github.com/sculptdotfun/viberank) (~115★, MIT) — public leaderboard on ccusage data.
- [851-labs/tokenmaxxing](https://github.com/851-labs/tokenmaxxing) (~68★, MIT) — sync personal ccusage with peers.
- [Nihondo/AgentLimits](https://github.com/Nihondo/AgentLimits), [kenn-io/vibepulse](https://github.com/kenn-io/vibepulse), [goniszewski/cctray](https://github.com/goniszewski/cctray), [sivchari/ccowl](https://github.com/sivchari/ccowl) — macOS menu-bar / widget wrappers around Claude/Codex usage.
- [atomchung/ccstory](https://github.com/atomchung/ccstory) (~43★) — narrative recap on top of ccusage bills.

**Build takeaways**
1. Treat **OpenUsage** as the collision product; **ccusage** as the report/parser standard to stay compatible with.
2. Differentiate on live quota probing + auto-detect reliability + dashboard UX, not raw historical report breadth.
3. Reuse LiteLLM pricing ideas; don't become a hosted OTel stack (Langfuse/Helicone stay adjacent).
4. Cursor Admin API / vendor `/usage` endpoints remain single-vendor — agentUsage's job is unification.

Sources: GitHub search/API 2026-09-04 (stars approximate).

5. Cursor Admin API trackers and single-vendor Codex/Claude desktop apps stay single-pane — agentUsage job is unification across every agent on the machine.

### Open source — 9 Oct 2026

Friday GitHub API pass (Europe/London), covering overnight and Friday morning since the 8 Oct entry. ccusage stays the format to be compatible with; the live-quota menu-bar layer (CodexBar, OpenUsage, ClaudeBar, codeburn, tokscale, AIUsage) keeps shipping daily. **Biggest movers:** `steipete/CodexBar` is now the clear category leader at **~22.3k★** and kept adding provider plugins plus native Codex tool-activity spend inspect on main; `tddworks/ClaudeBar` shipped **v0.5.11**; `seakee/CPA-Manager-Plus` shipped **v1.14.5**; `fschmutz/claude-usage-panel` raced **v3.1 → v3.6.0** overnight.

- [leeguooooo/claude-code-usage-bar](https://github.com/leeguooooo/claude-code-usage-bar) (~378★, MIT) — **v3.46.1**: `cs doctor` shows auto-upgrade / latest; failed refresh keeps last good per-model limits.

- [itvincent-git/codex-usage-desktop](https://github.com/itvincent-git/codex-usage-desktop) (~47★, MIT) — hold **app-v3.13.2**; preserve archived session usage.
- [alibaba/loongsuite-pilot](https://github.com/alibaba/loongsuite-pilot) (~200★, Apache-2.0) — still shipping Qoder IDE SQLite token parsing; same problem class as Cursor's state DB.
- Micro Cursor/Claude menu-bar apps keep spawning — noise floor next to CodexBar's breadth.

**Build takeaways:** treat CodexBar as the macOS ceiling to differentiate against (terminal + Linux/Windows + API keys/gateways). Match ClaudeBar's per-model daily chart and discover Codex auth from the Keychain as well as `auth.json`. Keep Cursor local dollars labelled as estimates; copy codeburn's project-link and first-run import clear. AIUsage's "reset windows show as full, not hidden" is a cheap honesty fix. Never blank limit UI on a single failed refresh (claude-code-usage-bar / ClaudeBar pattern).

Sources: GitHub API and GitHub search, 2026-10-09 (Europe/London).

Count Codex Auto-review as free from 6 Oct (OpenUsage + pane). Collapse finished sessions into counts and show session titles under repos (ClaudeBar). Keep Claude 5.5 reasoning-tier aliases mapped in the price table (tokscale). Survive provider DB migrations without dropping usage (splitrail 3.11.1).

**Build takeaways:** price free Codex Auto-review requests at **$0** unless the user sets an explicit rate, and keep them off list prices (CodexBar #4453). Never let a background usage probe open a browser sign-in (#4451). Bill reasoning tokens once, count Claude advisor usage, and read zstd Codex transcripts (tokscale). Group subagents and delegated runs under their parent session, and group Cursor Grok Bot records separately (token-monitor 0.69.0, Syrtis). Ask for Keychain access only after the user picks providers, and keep Always Allow sticky (OpenUsage 0.7.15-beta.1). Show sub-cent costs rather than $0.00 (Langfuse #18582), and forecast "runs out before reset" per window (QuotaBar 0.6.0). A localhost web view over the same SQLite (splitrail #276) is now table stakes next to the TUI.

Sources: GitHub API and GitHub search, 2026-10-12 (Europe/London).

**Build takeaways:** offer a graphical progress bar for each quota window (session/weekly/scoped) and keep provider errors on a separate line from "showing last-known usage" notices (CodexBar #4436/#4496). Let users filter/search/group providers and **reversibly restore catalog order** (#4440). When counting Copilot/Cursor agent chats, prefer request totals / `modelTotals` over last-round metadata so multi-round turns aren't undercounted (codeburn #1752). Recognise Codex workspace `spend_control` limits and never mask a triggered limit with a healthier 5h/weekly balance (CPA 1.14.6). Share read-only quota snapshots over Tailscale/LAN with a token instead of logging the same account into a second machine (AIUsage 0.15.26). Give CLI-discovery shells a hard deadline so Syncing… can't stick forever (ClaudeBar 0.5.15). Read vendor percentages from the vendor's own limits endpoint rather than re-deriving them from a charge ledger (ai-usagebar ClinePass). Compare dashboard readouts to prior days (token-meter) and keep a localhost web view over the same SQLite next to the TUI.

Sources: GitHub API and GitHub search, 2026-10-13 (Europe/London).

- [HVEPYC/claude-code-usage-limit-checker](https://github.com/HVEPYC/claude-code-usage-limit-checker) (~1★, MIT, created 13 Oct) — **new**: exposes Claude usage limits **as an MCP tool** so the agent itself can check its headroom. Worth considering as an agentUsage MCP surface.
- [splunk/token-meter](https://github.com/splunk/token-meter) (~119★, MIT) — Tue: **reads OpenCode V2 `session_v2`/`session_message` storage** (#77), every date filter defaults to 30 days, readouts compare to prior days.
- [beboite/douane](https://github.com/beboite/douane) (~1★, AGPL-3.0) — Wed: quota reads retry with a widening delay and **show errors only when they last** (#3); reserve counts only the account's longest window (#4).
- [itvincent-git/codex-usage-desktop](https://github.com/itvincent-git/codex-usage-desktop) (~51★, MIT) — **app-v3.15.2** (Tue): replay fixes for mixed tool batches.
- [alibaba/loongsuite-pilot](https://github.com/alibaba/loongsuite-pilot) (~203★, Apache-2.0) — Wed: install-time workspace collection **blocklist** (#434).
- [majiayu000/quotabar](https://github.com/majiayu000/quotabar), [fschmutz/claude-usage-panel](https://github.com/fschmutz/claude-usage-panel), [leeguooooo/claude-code-usage-bar](https://github.com/leeguooooo/claude-code-usage-bar), [ItsJazii/pane](https://github.com/ItsJazii/pane), [Dicklesworthstone/coding_agent_usage_tracker](https://github.com/Dicklesworthstone/coding_agent_usage_tracker) — quiet.

**Build takeaways:** tail growing session logs from the last read offset instead of re-parsing whole files — OpenUsage's 94 s → 0.44 s is the benchmark (#1383), and skip oversized records rather than crashing sync (TokenTracker #790). Store prices with **effective-from dates** so intro rates expire on schedule (splitrail #279, Gemini 4 Argon). Return an empty report, not an error, when a provider's data directory doesn't exist (ccusage #1875). Show "has tokens but no price" as a distinct no-cost state instead of $0 (langfuse #18686), and flag cost reports built from incomplete history (CodexBar #4472). Bound credential/Keychain prompts and rate-limit OAuth quota reads (CodexBar 0.75.0, ai-usagebar Muse Code). Consider an MCP tool that lets an agent ask agentUsage how much headroom it has left (HVEPYC), and say whether a headline total includes cache tokens (magpie Σ).

Sources: GitHub API and GitHub search, 2026-10-14 (Europe/London).

### Open source — 15 Oct 2026

Thursday GitHub API pass (Europe/London), covering Wednesday morning through Thursday morning since the 14 Oct entry. ccusage stays the format to be compatible with; the live-quota menu-bar layer (CodexBar, OpenUsage, ClaudeBar, codeburn, tokscale, AIUsage, Syrtis, ai-usagebar, token-monitor, TokenTracker) keeps shipping daily. **Biggest movers:** Codex rollout files can now arrive **compressed**, and both `ccusage/ccusage` **v20.0.33** (reads Zstandard-compressed rollouts, #1890) and `junhoyeo/tokscale` **v4.19.1** (keeps usage from large compressed rollouts, #1453; counts compaction turns, #1439) had to catch up; `steipete/CodexBar` main now **fetches provider usage in parallel in the CLI** (#4501) and adds **Claude Platform API credits** (#4510); and a new crop of quota tools exposes limits **to the agents themselves** (dnlbit/usagebar's MCP `get_usage`, lamtuanvu/pacer's usage-paced routing).

[majiayu000/quotabar](https://github.com/majiayu000/quotabar), [fschmutz/claude-usage-panel](https://github.com/fschmutz/claude-usage-panel), [leeguooooo/claude-code-usage-bar](https://github.com/leeguooooo/claude-code-usage-bar), [sandrich/headroom](https://github.com/sandrich/headroom), [HVEPYC/claude-code-usage-limit-checker](https://github.com/HVEPYC/claude-code-usage-limit-checker), [beboite/douane](https://github.com/beboite/douane), [alibaba/loongsuite-pilot](https://github.com/alibaba/loongsuite-pilot) — quiet.

**Build takeaways:** the Codex parser must handle **Zstandard-compressed rollouts** and **compaction turns** now, or totals silently drop (ccusage #1890, tokscale #1453/#1439) — add a fixture for each. Prefer the freshest credential source when a Keychain login and a credentials file disagree (tokscale #1401). Fetch providers in parallel and put a hard time bound on every provider CLI, even past SIGKILL (CodexBar #4501, ClaudeBar #561). Make Cursor sync timeouts scale with history size (tokscale #1417). Pace arrows (usage minus elapsed share of the window) are a compact way to show burn rate in a TUI (ai-usagebar 1.38.0). An MCP `get_usage` tool for agents is now a recurring idea (usagebar, HVEPYC) — worth shipping early.

Sources: GitHub API and GitHub search, 2026-10-15 (Europe/London).

**Build takeaways:** read Cursor's unified plan, prepaid balance and current-period included usage, not just request counts (token-monitor #1056/#1073). Attach cost provenance (which price table, which model mapping, estimated or billed) to every aggregate we export (tokscale #1422). De-duplicate Copilot OTEL snapshots that appear in more than one file (tokscale #1434). Bucket days in the user's time zone (ClaudeBar #565). Show sub-cent costs as `<$0.01` rather than rounding to zero (tokscale #1420). Profile plugin/provider memory over many refreshes and never overwrite a config that failed to load (CodexBar v0.76.0).

Sources: GitHub API and GitHub search, 2026-10-16 (Europe/London).

### Open source — 17 Oct 2026

They look like a copy-repo campaign; don't install from them, and expect the same to happen to agentUsage once it gets attention (pin the official repo and npm package name in our README).
- [851-labs/tokenmaxxing](https://github.com/851-labs/tokenmaxxing), [Nihondo/AgentLimits](https://github.com/Nihondo/AgentLimits), [sylearn/AIUsage](https://github.com/sylearn/AIUsage) (~725★), [leeguooooo/claude-code-usage-bar](https://github.com/leeguooooo/claude-code-usage-bar), [majiayu000/quotabar](https://github.com/majiayu000/quotabar), [mryll/claudebar](https://github.com/mryll/claudebar), [Dicklesworthstone/coding_agent_usage_tracker](https://github.com/Dicklesworthstone/coding_agent_usage_tracker), [Helicone/helicone](https://github.com/Helicone/helicone) (~6.2k★), [Elvis-codeur/django-llm-ledger](https://github.com/Elvis-codeur/django-llm-ledger) — quiet since the 16 Oct entry.

**Build takeaways:** make sure our probes and watchers don't keep the machine awake or burn idle CPU, and measure both (CodexBar v0.77.0). Never show 0 tokens for an interval we haven't read yet; show it as still loading (Syrtis #509). Let a fresh current-session reading override older totals, including a correct zero (token-monitor #1071). Use each provider's own context window and pricing table rather than models.dev defaults (magpie on Codex 272K, LiteLLM #47085 for Copilot). Attribute usage to projects from each record's working directory (TokenTracker #822), and when a session mixes models, split tokens by model (agent-standup v1.37.0). Show staleness on every quota reading and let users choose a 15/30/60-minute refresh (ai-usagebar v1.39.0). Pin our official repo and package name prominently, given the `toktrack` copy wave.

Sources: GitHub API and GitHub search, 2026-10-17 (Europe/London).

### Open source — 18 Oct 2026

Sunday GitHub API pass (Europe/London), covering Saturday morning through Sunday morning since the 17 Oct entry. ccusage stays the format to be compatible with; the live-quota layer (CodexBar, OpenUsage, ClaudeBar, codeburn, tokscale, token-monitor, TokenTracker, ai-usagebar, Syrtis) keeps shipping daily. **Biggest movers:** `junhoyeo/tokscale` **v4.20.0** plus Saturday-evening merges that **split Cursor plan usage into separate Auto and API tracks** and **import a signed-in Cursor desktop session automatically** (#1403, #1397), directly on agentUsage's Cursor ground; `ccusage` **v20.0.35** now reports **stored cost in JSON model breakdowns**; Codex's **compressed rollout archives (`.jsonl.zst`)** are now read by both TokenTracker and tokscale; and a new privacy-first meter, `KNIGHT-AI-AV/ringside`, reads only local usage files and never the provider login.

**New or newly noticed this pass:**
- [KNIGHT-AI-AV/ringside](https://github.com/KNIGHT-AI-AV/ringside) (7★, MIT, C#, created 15 Oct) — always-on-top Claude Code + Codex limit meter for Windows/macOS/Linux that **reads only the usage numbers apps already save locally and never touches the stored login token**; card / orb / pill views, "fill up" vs "fuel tank" display, forecasts and alerts. Its no-credentials stance is a strong selling point we can match.
- [Hug0x0/burnless](https://github.com/Hug0x0/burnless) (1★, MIT, Swift, created 17 Oct) — native macOS dashboard for Claude Code and Codex (API-equivalent spend, plan value, limits with pace and forecast) **built on ccusage as its data engine**; a fork of Agent Burn. More evidence that ccusage JSON is the de facto interchange.
- [HY916-cn/ClaudeMeter](https://github.com/HY916-cn/ClaudeMeter) (2★, MIT, created 17 Oct) — macOS menu bar that gets Claude limits by **driving the local Claude CLI's `/usage`** from a dedicated trusted directory; ported from CodexMeter.
- [flyresearch/agent-usage](https://github.com/flyresearch/agent-usage) (1★, MIT, Rust, created 14 Oct) — collector for Codex, Claude Code, Antigravity, Hermes and OpenClaw that exports **versioned JSON snapshots with explicit coverage**, reports "known usage, not billing", and does no default home-dir discovery. Note the name overlap with ours.
- [xAerux/aeru-codex-usage-hud](https://github.com/xAerux/aeru-codex-usage-hud) (5★), [garycny/CodexUsageMenu](https://github.com/garycny/CodexUsageMenu), [SuKiWSN/claude-codex-usage](https://github.com/SuKiWSN/claude-codex-usage) (GNOME top bar), [saimwankhaung/shangyi-usage](https://github.com/saimwankhaung/shangyi-usage), [TedoNeObichaJavaScript/ClawdyCalc](https://github.com/TedoNeObichaJavaScript/ClawdyCalc) — more 0–5★ single-provider widgets from 14–17 Oct; the long tail keeps growing.

**Build takeaways:** for Cursor, show Auto and API usage as separate tracks and read the desktop session from `state.vscdb` when the user hasn't logged in to us, with a full date window on the usage request (tokscale #1403/#1397). Read Codex's compressed `.jsonl.zst` rollouts and compaction turns, or totals come out low (TokenTracker #829, tokscale v4.20.0). Include stored cost in our JSON per-model breakdown so ccusage consumers can switch to us without changes (ccusage v20.0.35). Label each quota gauge with its window length and never classify quota buckets by id prefix alone (Syrtis v2.9.0, ai-usagebar v1.40.0). Honour `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`. State plainly in the README that we read local files and never send or reuse the provider login (ringside).

Sources: GitHub API and GitHub search, 2026-10-18 (Europe/London).
